8. EVIDENTIARY RULE
============================================================
A valid promotion finding requires:

  exact specification identity
        +
  explicit provenance/status event
        +
  connection between the event and MERKLE-V1

The following are NOT sufficient by themselves:
  - generic architectural freeze
  - successful implementation tests
  - vector conformance
  - odd-node 'promoted' construction language
  - presence of the word FROZEN in Section 16
